The phase table above is the only active remaining-work table in this ledger.
Completed rows are removed from it after the full row scope and required
evidence pass. The change log below is append-only audit history and must not be
read as current remaining work. A passing code batch closes the matching active
sub-item only. It must not keep completed implementation work hidden inside a
broad parent row; any still-open runtime, scale, or recovery evidence must be
listed as its own row.
